- **Cancer Biologist Expert Agent**: Specialized expert agent operating as a Cancer Biologist with full theoretical and practical domain reasoning. ([Tashima-Tarsh/Disha6.6](https://github.com/Tashima-Tarsh/Disha6.6/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/agent-cancer-biologist))
- **cancer-associated-fibroblast-subtype-annotation**: Use when a scRNA-seq stromal/CAF clustering partition contains low-ROGUE (impure) clusters and finer subtypes — such as myCAF splits or CD74/MHC-II+ apCAFs — must be resolved and annotated. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cancer-associated-fibroblast-subtype-annotation))
- **cancer-biologist**: Think and work like an expert Cancer Biologist. Use when a task calls for Cancer Biologist judgment. Reasons from hallmark capabilities, clonal evolution, and TME context; separates driver from passenger, cell-autonomous from stromal mechanisms, and 2D artifacts from PDO/PDX-validated dependencies using TCGA, DepMap, OncoKB, and REMARK-grade biomarker logic. ([K-Dense-AI/scientific-agents](https://github.com/K-Dense-AI/scientific-agents/tree/HEAD/scientific-agents/cancer-biologist/skills/cancer-biologist) · ★255 · 2 copies)
- **cancer-biologist**: Expert-thinking profile for Cancer Biologist (wet-lab / cancer genomics / preclinical oncology): Reasons from hallmark capabilities, clonal evolution, and TME context; separates driver from passenger, cell-autonomous from stromal mechanisms, and 2D artifacts from PDO/PDX-validated dependencies using TCGA, DepMap, OncoKB, and REMARK- grade biomarker logic. ([stanfish06/skillquarium](https://github.com/stanfish06/skillquarium/tree/HEAD/skills/cancer-biologist) · ★13)
- **cancer-biologist**: Reasons from hallmark capabilities, clonal evolution, and TME context; separates driver from passenger, cell-autonomous from stromal mechanisms, and 2D artifacts from PDO/PDX-validated dependencies using TCGA, DepMap, OncoKB, and REMARK-grade biomarker logic. ([raktim-mondol/hermes-plugin-researchcraft](https://github.com/raktim-mondol/hermes-plugin-researchcraft/tree/HEAD/skills/cancer-biologist) · ★5 · 3 copies)
- **cancer-biologist**: Expert instructions and domain workflows for cancer biologist. ([Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills](https://github.com/Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills/tree/HEAD/awesome_skills/engineering/cancer-biologist))
- **cancer-biomarker-candidate-identification**: Use when tumor vs non-tumor RNA-Seq expression data with sample metadata (tumor status, organ) must be mined for differentially expressed genes, and candidates should be triaged against The Human Protein Atlas prognostic-marker flags. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/cancer-biomarker-candidate-identification))
- **cancer-biomarker-signature-generation**: Use when raw TCGA-style RNA-Seq gene count matrices for paired normal vs cancer samples (e.g., KIRC, LIHC, PRAD) must be turned into a differential-expression baseline signature to benchmark other gene sets by classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cancer-biomarker-signature-generation))
- **cancer-buddy**: 抗癌搭子（cancer-buddy）是肿瘤患者和获授权照护者的非临床导航入口：整理病历、解释稳定概念、实时联网查证并解释权威指南/标准治疗的一般情况（带来源）、生成就诊问题、照护/饮食支持、第二意见资料包、资源和病例文献检索。它不对你个人下诊断、分期/ECOG/疗效/进展/预后判决，也不替你做个体化治疗/换线决策——指南能查、能讲“一般怎么治”，但你该用哪个方案由主诊医生定。 Triggers on 抗癌搭子, 搭子, 患者导航, 帮我分析病情, 刚确诊, 病历整理, 家属, 陪护, burnout, 吃什么, 忌口, 第二意见, 跨境会诊, 告不告诉, 不想让对方知道, 数据保险箱, 宣教手册, 找医院, 找医生, MDT, 临床试验, 就诊准备, 复诊, 看医生, 严不严重, 能治好吗, 预后, 找病例报告. ([CancerDAO/cancer-buddy-skill](https://github.com/CancerDAO/cancer-buddy-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/cancer-buddy) · ★6)
- **cancer-buddy-caregiver**: 为癌症患者的照护者提供非临床的陪诊准备、家庭分工、儿童沟通和照护负担支持。访问患者资料需要明确授权；不提供临床判断或固定化疗护理参数。Triggers on 家属, 陪护, 照护者, burnout, 怎么陪诊, 家庭分工, 怎么告诉孩子. ([CancerDAO/cancer-buddy-skill](https://github.com/CancerDAO/cancer-buddy-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/cancer-buddy-caregiver) · ★6)
- **cancer-buddy-case-precedent**: 检索 PubMed、Europe PMC 等来源中的病例报告，完整呈现病例差异、治疗经过和全部结局，作为不可推广的研究线索。无相似度分数或排序，不生成治疗方向、适用性、获益概率或患者预后。Triggers on 相似病例, 病例报告, case report, 真实病例, 文献先例. ([CancerDAO/cancer-buddy-skill](https://github.com/CancerDAO/cancer-buddy-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/cancer-buddy-case-precedent) · ★6)
- **cancer-buddy-charts**: 把患者自己的检验、治疗、分子与资料数据画成可打印、可转发、可带去问医生的静态图表。只呈现源报告里已有的数值，不解读趋势含义、不判断疗效或病情。当用户问某个具体指标的情况、要求画图/趋势图，或任何 cancer-buddy 子技能需要在回答里附一张图时使用。Triggers on 画图, 趋势图, 图表, 可视化, 曲线, 变化趋势, 我的 CEA 怎么样, 指标变化, chart, plot, trend。 ([CancerDAO/cancer-buddy-skill](https://github.com/CancerDAO/cancer-buddy-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/cancer-buddy-charts) · ★6)
- **cancer-buddy-disclosure**: 帮助癌症患者、照护者和家庭准备知情沟通，记录患者的信息偏好，并在能力或代理权不确定时路由医疗团队/伦理/法律支持。不会由模型判定能力或维持欺骗。Triggers on 要不要告诉, 瞒着癌症, 患者问是不是癌症, 知情同意, disclosure. ([CancerDAO/cancer-buddy-skill](https://github.com/CancerDAO/cancer-buddy-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/cancer-buddy-disclosure) · ★6)
- **cancer-buddy-education**: 生成来源可追溯的肿瘤患者教育材料，解释稳定概念并把个体问题整理给主诊团队。可在实时核验现行一手来源后转述指南对“这类情况”的一般推荐（含具体方案/线次名称，带来源），但不替患者选定方案。指南、药品标签、获批状态、预后数字、随访和急诊阈值必须在回答时从版本可核验的一手来源读取：可用用户合法持有的现行指南并标版本/页码，否则实时查官方来源；不可用时失败关闭，不以模型记忆兜底。Triggers on 患者教育, 宣教手册, 看懂癌症, 看懂治疗, NCCN, CSCO, 指南建议, 严不严重, 能治好吗. ([CancerDAO/cancer-buddy-skill](https://github.com/CancerDAO/cancer-buddy-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/cancer-buddy-education) · ★6)
- **cancer-buddy-find-care**: 查找可核验的肿瘤医院、医生、服务和临床试验站点资源。只做资源发现与联系方式核验，输出不排序的候选清单；不评价医疗质量，不推荐医生/医院，不判断试验资格或治疗适用性。Triggers on 找医院, 找医生, 找专家, MTB, MDT, NGS, 临床试验在哪招, 异地就医. ([CancerDAO/cancer-buddy-skill](https://github.com/CancerDAO/cancer-buddy-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/cancer-buddy-find-care) · ★6)
- **cancer-buddy-nutrition**: Provide symptom-directed nutrition education, food-safety guidance, and pharmacist/dietitian routing for people affected by cancer. Use for questions about eating, treatment-related nutrition symptoms, food/drug interactions, supplements, and culturally familiar meal ideas. Never prescribe nutrient targets or infer interactions from model memory. ([CancerDAO/cancer-buddy-skill](https://github.com/CancerDAO/cancer-buddy-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/cancer-buddy-nutrition) · ★6)
- **cancer-buddy-organize**: Organize patient medical records into a provenance-preserving patient directory. Produces source-attributed summaries and schema-validated JSON while keeping source-reported, patient-reported and normalized layers separate. It does not infer diagnosis, stage, ECOG, response, progression, treatment line, prognosis or testing indications. Triggers on 病历整理, 整理报告, organize medical records. ([CancerDAO/cancer-buddy-skill](https://github.com/CancerDAO/cancer-buddy-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/cancer-buddy-organize) · ★6)
- **cancer-buddy-second-opinion**: 从患者已整理资料生成来源可追溯的二次意见资料包、记录索引和问题清单。仅转录与组织，不推断诊断、分期、ECOG、疗效、进展或治疗选择；跨境联系和材料要求必须实时核验。Triggers on 第二意见, second opinion, 海外会诊, 病例摘要, 会诊资料包. ([CancerDAO/cancer-buddy-skill](https://github.com/CancerDAO/cancer-buddy-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/cancer-buddy-second-opinion) · ★6)
- **cancer-buddy-vault**: 为患者的本地肿瘤资料建立清单、访问政策、导出与审计流程。分享必须依赖宿主鉴权和患者的明确、可撤销、限目的授权；不把 patient_code 或亲属关系当权限。Triggers on 数据保险箱, 健康档案, 数据分享, 导出病历, 撤销授权. ([CancerDAO/cancer-buddy-skill](https://github.com/CancerDAO/cancer-buddy-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/cancer-buddy-vault) · ★6)
- **cancer-buddy-visit-prep**: Assemble a one-page 就诊准备包 (visit prep pack) from an organized patient_dir — doctor's 30-second snapshot, the questions to ask the doctor, what to bring, and (follow-up only) what changed since last visit. Only assembles existing data + organizes questions; no treatment advice, no result interpretation, no clinical judgment. Triggers on 就诊准备, 明天看医生, 复诊准备, 该问医生什么, visit prep. ([CancerDAO/cancer-buddy-skill](https://github.com/CancerDAO/cancer-buddy-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/cancer-buddy-visit-prep) · ★6)
- **cancer-cell**: Use when targeting Cancer Cell or deciding whether a cancer-biology manuscript fits this venue. Encodes the journal's fit, framing, method-and-evidence bar, house style, official-submission re-check, and desk-reject heuristics. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/English-NaturalScience-Journal-Skills/skills/cancer-cell) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **cancer-cell-line-data-processing**: Use when a pancancer expression matrix from cell line models (e.g., DepMap/GDSC/NCI60) must be joined to cancer-type metadata and summarized per cancer type for a specific gene such as TP53. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/cancer-cell-line-data-processing))
- **cancer-cell-line-drug-response-analysis**: Use when you have matched gene expression profiles for cancer cell lines plus drug sensitivity (IC50) data (e.g., the GDSC panel) and want to identify which signaling pathway activities explain drug response. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cancer-cell-line-drug-response-analysis))
- **cancer-cell-line-expression-data-harmonization**: Use when a bulk cancer cell-line expression matrix (GDSC Affymetrix U219 microarray, CCLE RSEM RNA-seq) must be log2(x+1)-normalized and aggregated over TMG/NMG gene sets for correlation with IC50 drug sensitivity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cancer-cell-line-expression-data-harmonization))
- **cancer-cell-of-origin-annotation**: Use when annotating epithelial cells in a cancer scRNA-seq dataset (e.g., liver, pancreatic, colon, kidney, lung, mammary tumors) to identify malignant cells and infer their cell of origin from a Seurat object with a UMAP reduction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cancer-cell-of-origin-annotation))
- **cancer-cohort-expression-profiling**: Use when you have a defined gene set (e.g. genes on an eccDNA like hsa_Chr7_3S_1) and expression matrices from tumor and matched normal samples, and need per-gene expression ranks and t-test p-values per cancer cohort. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cancer-cohort-expression-profiling))
- **cancer-curator**: Skill for curating cancer and neoplastic disease entries in the dismech knowledge base. Use this skill when creating or enhancing cancer entries, adding oncogene/tumor suppressor pathophysiology, curating histopathology findings, adding targeted therapy information, and linking genetic drivers to treatment responses. Covers fusion genes, two-hit hypothesis, oncogene addiction, and precision oncology concepts. ([monarch-initiative/dismech](https://github.com/monarch-initiative/dismech/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/cancer-curator) · ★73 · 2 copies)
- **cancer-database-annotation-comparison**: Use when evaluating a pretrained malignant-cell annotation model (Cancer-Finder, checkpoints/sc_pretrain_article.pkl) against database-curated tumor annotations across many scRNA-seq datasets to test generalization at scale. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cancer-database-annotation-comparison))
- **cancer-discovery**: Use when targeting Cancer Discovery (AACR) or deciding whether a cancer biology or translational oncology manuscript fits this venue. Encodes the journal's fit, framing, method-and-evidence bar, house style, official-submission re-check, and desk-reject heuristics. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/English-NaturalScience-Journal-Skills/skills/cancer-discovery) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **cancer-driver-benchmark-method-comparison**: Use when comparing predicted pan-cancer driver genes from cTaG (or similar RandomForest-based TSG/OG classifiers) against established tools like TUSON, 20/20+ and DriverNet using curated driver lists such as Bailey et al. and CGC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cancer-driver-benchmark-method-comparison))
- **cancer-driver-call-overlap-analysis**: Use when benchmarking a driver discovery method's called elements against a gold-standard set (e.g. CGC) on PCAWG whole genomes, to compute precision, recall and F1 per element class (coding, splice site, non-coding). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cancer-driver-call-overlap-analysis))
- **cancer-driver-candidate-comparison-against-reference-catalogs**: Use when you have a tool-called driver candidate list (e.g. DriverPower [outDir]/[name].result.tsv) and need to benchmark its novelty and error rate against CGC or PCAWG-consensus catalogs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cancer-driver-candidate-comparison-against-reference-catalogs))
- **cancer-driver-gene-benchmark-scoring**: Use when ranking driver-gene prediction lists (your own or tools like PNC, DawnRank, MutSigCV) against curated CGC, NCG or PMH benchmark gene sets with precision, recall and a precision-weighted F0.2 score across TCGA cancer types. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cancer-driver-gene-benchmark-scoring))
- **cancer-driver-gene-classification**: Use when genes must be classified cancer driver vs non-driver from multi-omics features averaged per gene over a directed GRN such as DawnNet, KEGG, or RegNetwork, e.g. to reproduce the DGMP 5-fold cross-validation benchmark. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cancer-driver-gene-classification--6d6ce0a7aff14890))
- **cancer-driver-gene-classification**: Use when somatic mutation data (e.g. COSMIC MAF-style coding mutations) must be converted into per-gene mutation-type features and genes classified as TSG vs OG, especially to nominate low-frequency candidate drivers among unlabelled genes without background-mutation-rate bias. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cancer-driver-gene-classification--bad5436a2eaef27b))
- **cancer-driver-gene-curation**: Use when a ranked candidate driver gene list (e.g., iEDGE Rank-1 cis genes per SCNA) needs in silico validation against curated oncogene/tumor suppressor compendia (COSMIC, TUSON, OMIM, UniProt) or DepMap dependency data via one-sided Fisher/hypergeometric enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cancer-driver-gene-curation))
- **cancer-driver-gene-ranking-evaluation**: Use when you have DGMP (or similar) gene-level driver-probability predictions (e.g., 'logits' from './code/result/') and need to assess whether top-ranked, unannotated predictions show cancer-like multi-omics alteration patterns versus known cancer genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cancer-driver-gene-ranking-evaluation))
- **cancer-driver-mutation-inference**: Use when you have processed somatic mutation response tables (nMut, nSample, effective length per element) for coding or non-coding test elements and a pre-trained BMR model, and need to call driver candidates at q &lt; 0.1. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/cancer-driver-mutation-inference))
- **cancer-driver-network-inference**: Use when the goal is to infer a context-specific cancer network from mutated seed genes and quantitatively compare driver-gene recovery (precision/recall vs IntOGen) across network inference methods under cross-validation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/cancer-driver-network-inference))
- **cancer-functional-genomics**: Analyze cancer dependency screens and functional-genomics datasets. Use for DepMap-like gene effects, cell-line metadata, lineage context, biomarker associations, confounding checks, and reproducible prioritization. ([anonymous-temp/EviMed-Science](https://github.com/anonymous-temp/EviMed-Science/tree/HEAD/OpenScience/runtime/skills/curated-scientific/cancer-functional-genomics) · ★1)
- **cancer-gene-census-reference-list-validation**: Use when a driver-gene prediction method must be benchmarked for precision against curated reference driver sets (Bailey et al. pan-cancer list or CGC), especially to rank methods like cTaG, TUSON, 20/20+ and DriverNet on the same evaluation genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cancer-gene-census-reference-list-validation))
- **cancer-gene-coexpression-analysis**: Use when a panel of cancer genes (e.g., 40 genes from &gt;10K TCGA pan-cancer samples) must be screened for co-expression and visualized as a clustered pairwise correlation heatmap. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cancer-gene-coexpression-analysis))
- **cancer-gene-expression-data-loading**: Use when reproducing the GNIAP gene co-expression network workflow and the study's breast or prostate cancer expression data files must be located, loaded, and fed into the GCN inference step in R. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cancer-gene-expression-data-loading))
- **cancer-genomics-analysis**: Computational cancer genomics workflows. Somatic mutation detection and annotation, structural variation characterization, copy number analysis, tumor purity/ploidy estimation, NMF metagene extraction, and DNA damage response network analysis. For cancer mutation databases use cosmic-database; for variant clinical significance use clinvar-database. ([synthetic-sciences/openscience](https://github.com/synthetic-sciences/openscience/tree/HEAD/backend/cli/skills/biology/cancer-genomics-analysis) · ★4.4k · 9 copies)
- **cancer-genomics-analysis**: Cancer genomics analysis — somatic variant interpretation, TMB, MSI, actionability, companion diagnostics, ctDNA. Triggers on somatic mutations, driver mutations, passenger mutations, TMB, tumor mutational burden, MSI, microsatellite instability, actionability, oncogenic variants, companion diagnostics, tumor profiling, liquid biopsy, ctDNA, OncoKB, CIViC, variant of unknown significance, VUS. ALSO ACTIVATE WHEN THE QUESTION NAMES A DATABASE AND LOOKS LIKE PLAIN RETRIEVAL — "what does COSMIC, cBioPortal or GDC say about X", "look up Y". A matching tool name is not a substitute for this skill:… ([mark64oswald/lexa-bioscience-public](https://github.com/mark64oswald/lexa-bioscience-public/tree/HEAD/skills/domains/cancer-genomics))
- **cancer-genomics-best-practices**: Use when applying cancer genomics best practices. ([LoopyLuci/Skills](https://github.com/LoopyLuci/Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/cancer-genomics-best-practices) · ★2)
- **cancer-genomics-evidence**: Synthesize public cancer genomic alterations across genes, cohorts, molecular profiles, clinical annotations, and literature. Use for somatic landscape, biomarker, resistance, or translational oncology research. ([eightmm/codex-science](https://github.com/eightmm/codex-science/tree/HEAD/authored-skills/cancer-genomics-evidence) · ★4)
- **cancer-genomics-fundamentals**: Use when applying cancer genomics fundamentals. ([LoopyLuci/Skills](https://github.com/LoopyLuci/Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/cancer-genomics-fundamentals) · ★2)
- **cancer-genomics-implementation**: Use when applying cancer genomics implementation. ([LoopyLuci/Skills](https://github.com/LoopyLuci/Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/cancer-genomics-implementation) · ★2)
- **cancer-genomics-significance-table-construction**: Use when a MAF-format mutation dataset must be tested per-gene between two sample groups defined by a continuous factor (e.g., hypoxia GSVA score strata) to yield a p-value/FDR significance table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cancer-genomics-significance-table-construction))
